Setting. P-S1: c = 4076476460239181/2251799813685248, q = 21/10, W^2 = 1 + 4|X|^2. The Fourier convention is the
nonunitary one, fhat(xi) = int exp(-i X.xi) f(X) dX, so int |fhat|^2 = (2 pi)^2 ||f||^2. J = <D>; R is the vector
Riesz transform, with sum_j ||R_j g||^2 = ||g||^2; v = R g; S = Sym D ubar; p(xi) = 1/<xi> and n(xi) = 1 - |xi|/<xi>.
Matrix Fourier magnitudes use the complex operator norm. Tests g are real Schwartz functions in the symmetry class.

Hypotheses. (H1) The background smoothness and decay needed for the admitted DR and KER identities (AT-332), by which

    T_IS(g) = -(q/2) (2 pi)^(-4) int int vhat(xi)^* Shat(xi - eta) vhat(eta) [(n(xi) - n(eta))^2 + (p(xi) - p(eta))^2] d eta d xi.

(H2) The moment

    M_S = (2 pi)^(-2) int |zeta|^2 (1 + 2 <zeta>^2) ||Shat(zeta)||_op d zeta

is finite.

Statement. Under (H1) and (H2), |T_IS(g)| <= (q/2) M_S ||J^(-1) g||^2 for every test g. The bound extends to the
completion in the norm ||J^(-1) .|| wherever the form is extended by continuity.

Proof. Put zeta = xi - eta. (i) The map x -> <x> is 1-Lipschitz, so |p(xi) - p(eta)| = |<eta> - <xi>| p(xi) p(eta) <=
|zeta| p(xi) p(eta). (ii) n(xi) = N(|xi|) with N(r) = 1 - r (1 + r^2)^(-1/2) and N'(r) = -(1 + r^2)^(-3/2), whose
absolute value decreases in r. The mean value theorem between |xi| and |eta|, with ||xi| - |eta|| <= |zeta|, gives
|n(xi) - n(eta)| <= |zeta| P^3, P = max(p(xi), p(eta)). (iii) Let P' = min(p(xi), p(eta)), so P P' = p(xi) p(eta).
Peetre's inequality <xi>^2 <= 2 <eta>^2 <xi - eta>^2 and its symmetric form give P/P' <= 2^(1/2) <zeta>. Since P <=
1, P^3 = (P/P') (P P') P <= 2^(1/2) <zeta> p(xi) p(eta). (iv) Hence (n(xi) - n(eta))^2 + (p(xi) - p(eta))^2 <=
|zeta|^2 (1 + 2 <zeta>^2) p(xi)^2 p(eta)^2 <= k(zeta) p(xi) p(eta), with k(zeta) = |zeta|^2 (1 + 2 <zeta>^2), since
p <= 1. (v) With |vhat(xi)^* Shat(zeta) vhat(eta)| <= |vhat(xi)| ||Shat(zeta)||_op |vhat(eta)| and w = p |vhat|,

    |T_IS(g)| <= (q/2) (2 pi)^(-4) int int w(xi) Kc(xi - eta) w(eta) d eta d xi,   Kc(zeta) = k(zeta) ||Shat(zeta)||_op >= 0.

Young's convolution inequality bounds the double integral by ||Kc||_1 ||w||_2^2, where ||Kc||_1 = (2 pi)^2 M_S and,
by Plancherel, ||w||_2^2 = int <xi>^(-2) |vhat(xi)|^2 d xi = (2 pi)^2 ||J^(-1) v||^2. The factors of 2 pi cancel. R
commutes with J and is a vector L^2 isometry, so ||J^(-1) v|| = ||J^(-1) g||. QED.

Corollary. Under the hypotheses of the Statement:
(a) Pointwise consequence. By Fourier inversion and 1 + 2 <zeta>^2 >= 3, sup_X ||Delta S(X)||_op <= (2 pi)^(-2) int
    |zeta|^2 ||Shat(zeta)||_op d zeta <= M_S/3. In the moments m_k(f) = (2 pi)^(-2) int |zeta|^k |fhat(zeta)| d zeta
    (operator norm for matrices), M_S = 3 m_2(S) + 2 m_4(S) (AT2463).
(b) Free-gain test. Under the whole-plane strain bound s_* <= 137/125 and the admitted far-piece hypotheses of
    Proposition FG (AT-513), the unused free gain is G - A_far = q (s_max - s_*) >= B =
    71675071190082633/5629499534213120000 (the second theory agent T2_CLOSURE_CHECK_v02 row B1). If (q/2) M_S <= B, that is M_S <=
    2B/q = 3413098628099173/281474976710656000 (about 0.0121257622; EXACT), then T_IS is paid from B alone. The test
    is sufficient; it is not equivalent to, and not necessary for, a fit of the actual form, and B is shared once by
    T_IS, C_tr and the C_vs pieces (AT-513; AT2441). Every level below 2B/q, for example 0.012125762, is a sufficient
    level. By (a) the test implies sup ||Delta S||_op <= 0.004041921 (rounded upward) on the whole plane.
(c) At 013. The numerical auditor's exact origin jets of the unwindowed Source013 velocity (numerical audit exchange v725; AT2456) give
    ||Delta S(0)||_op >= 1899576001577/10^12, which needs only the admitted smooth source, and, with finite Fourier
    moments and valid nonunitary inverse derivative formulas, M_S >= 5698728004731/10^12 (AT2457). Then (q/2) M_S >
    5.98 > B, so the test (b) fails at 013 (AT2458). This excludes that sufficient estimate against that budget only;
    it does not refute the actual T_IS form, a sharper estimate, a near and far split, H4 or existence (AT2459 OPEN).

Remarks.
1. The KER representation is linear in Shat, so the lemma applies to each piece S_j of a split S = S_1 + S_2 for
   which (H1) and (H2) hold, with M_(S_j) in place of M_S (elementary; CONFIRMED in AT2600). Since (b) fails at 013,
   the route proposed here uses such a split: the far part by the lemma with the moments of (1 - chi) S, the near
   part by Lemma HI' (high part, into the rate room; NR-34) and an NE-lo bound (low part, into the price allowance;
   theory_v21 V21-1 (d)). The failure of the unsplit sufficient test does not make a split necessary, and no other
   estimate is excluded by it (AT2605).
2. No upper of M_S, m_2(S) or m_4(S) at 013 is certified at this version. The first numerics agent's screen on core 44 and the second numerics agent's on
   core 46, in the normalizations of theory_agent_1/research/h4rest/SCREEN_specification_v01.md (AT2463), are HEURISTIC until a
   containing evaluation is supplied. The final FLOAT evaluations of the dilated Lemma MOM expressions (Remark 3;
   numerics_v17 N49.4) are 10.6808 for m_2(S), 24130.88 for m_4(S) and 48293.80 for M_S; they are not certified
   uppers (AT2590 OPEN).
3. Lemma MOM (T2_H4_v01; AT-363) bounds each moment by an L^2 norm of derivatives: m_k(S) <= (4 pi)^(-1/2) || |D|^k
   (1 - Delta) S ||_2 at eps = 1, which is how a certified upper of M_S would be obtained. For the stream function
   Psi of ubar = (-Psi_y, Psi_x), the symbol identity of NR-34 Corollary (c) gives m_2(S) = m_4(Psi)/2 and m_4(S) =
   m_6(Psi)/2 (numerical audit exchange v761; AT2564), and with C = (4 pi)^(-1/2), P4 = ||Delta^2 (1 - Delta) Psi||_2 and P6 =
   ||Delta^3 (1 - Delta) Psi||_2 Lemma MOM gives u_3 <= C P4, m_2(S) <= C P4/2 and m_4(S) <= C P6/2 when these norms
   are finite (AT2567). No value of P4 or P6 at 013 is certified.
4. Seventh-order origin jets (version 24; numerical audit exchange v785, adopted in v799; the numerical auditor
   research/source013_rest_seventh_jets/PROOF.md). With Phi = partial_y chi + phi, Psi = partial_x Phi and ubar =
   (-Psi_y, Psi_x) as there, let Phi_7 = A_7 x^6 y + B_7 x^4 y^3 + C_7 x^2 y^5 + D_7 y^7 (A, B, C and D there) be the
   homogeneous seventh-degree part of the Taylor expansion of Phi at the origin and J7 = 720 A_7 + 288 B_7 + 240 C_7.
   Then Delta^2 S(0) = diag(-J7, J7) (AT2622, AT2623), and |J7| lies in [2630971728787147/500000000000,
   1052388691514859/200000000000], so between 5261.943457574294 and 5261.943457574295 (AT2623). By Fourier inversion
   as in Corollary (a), ||Delta S(0)||_op <= m_2(S) and ||Delta^2 S(0)||_op <= m_4(S). Under the finite-moment and
   inversion hypotheses of Corollary (c) this gives m_4(S) >= 5261.943457574294 and, by Corollary (a), M_S = 3
   m_2(S) + 2 m_4(S) >= 263239641078833/25000000000 = 10529.58564315332 (the numerical auditor's directed display; AT2624). Then
   (q/2) M_S >= 11056.06, which exceeds B by a factor above 8.68e5, so the test (b) fails at 013 by that factor. This
   excludes the same sufficient estimate against the same budget and nothing else; Corollary (c) is unchanged, and
   whether this moment obstruction settles the actual form, H4-REST, an inverse or existence is OPEN (AT2625).

## NR-34. T1-Rule-HIcut: the least cut for the Lemma HI' upper to fit a rate budget (PROVED: AT2439, AT2464)

Status. PROVED: the theory auditor CONFIRMS (i) as a necessary condition for the nonnegative HI' sufficient upper to fit the rate
room (AT2439; research/h4rest_plan_v01_audit, audit_v746) and (ii) with the exact K (AT2464;
research/h4_screen_and_origin_cutoffs_audit, audit_v752). The values of Corollary (a) are CONFIRMED as directed
displays (AT2465; AT2496 at the (R4) version 2 tuple, audit_v760; AT2510 and AT2542 at the version 1 tuple,
audit_v764 and audit_v773). The theory auditor CONFIRMS the symbol identity and the origin lower of Corollary (c) (AT2564), the
T_IS coefficient q/2 of Corollary (d) in Lemma HI' for a planar divergence-free background (AT2565), and all 24
enclosures and 256 cut decisions of the first theory agent's hi_kq_v01 to hi_kq_v03 together with the K_half values of (d) by an
independent 512-bit evaluation (AT2566; research/stream_strain_hi_cutoffs_audit, audit_v780). The theory auditor's audit of
version 22 CONFIRMS NR-34 with the planar divergence-free restriction for K_half, a byte-identical replay of
hi_kq_v04 and an independent 512-bit evaluation of its four directed cut intervals and 48 decisions (AT2601), and the
dilated Lemma MOM of Remark 3 (AT2588, AT2602; research/tnr_v22_integration_audit, audit_v789). AT2606 and AT2607
REFUTE two wordings of version 22 (the heading of Corollary (c) and a blanket run-time statement); both were
corrected at version 23, and the theory auditor CONFIRMS the corrections (AT2632, AT2633; research/tnr_v23_corrections_audit,
audit_v797); no value changes. Remark 5 (version 24) records the second theory agent's directed u_3 thresholds (AT2658). Remark 6
(version 25): AT2721 and AT2757 CONFIRMED, AT2720 REFUTED, AT2722 OPEN; at version 27 AT2811 and AT2812 CONFIRMED,
AT2813 OPEN. Remark 7 (version 26): AT2752 to AT2754, AT2774 and AT2775 CONFIRMED, AT2755 and AT2776 OPEN. Remark 8
(version 27): AT2888 CONFIRMED, AT2889 OPEN (audit_v877); the numerical auditor's independent check CONFIRMS the same values (the numerical auditor
exchange v921); at version 28 AT2906 CONFIRMED (audit_v882). AT2568 REFUTES two literal statements of the first numerics agent's 10:23Z
note (the origin display is a strict lower, not an equality; 140.055012371285 is the version 1 cut with the axis
lower, not the multiplier (phi K/R)^(1/2)), without changing the lower of (c). AT2466, AT2511, AT2569 and AT2608 are
OPEN: these tests give no actual Fourier upper and do not settle the actual H4 form, H4-REST, closure or existence.
Stated in theory_agent_1/research/h4rest/H4REST_PLAN_v01.md section 4, the first theory agent's note 98 and theory_v21 V21-1 (e); Corollaries (b)
and (c) in the first theory agent's notes 102 and 103.

Purpose. The H4 rate budget left for all full-L^2 rest losses is R = beta_* - b0 - e_up (the rate room (A) of
theory_v21 V21-1 (c), at eps_rest = 0). Lemma HI' charges the high-frequency part of T_IS + C_tr to it. The rule
turns a certified lower of one moment of ubar into a lower for every cut at which that upper can fit.

Imported. Lemma HI' of the second theory agent (T2_H4_v01 section 3; CONFIRMED at AT-362 conditional on the complete coefficient
Fourier moments and tail bounds; imported, not re-proved), restricted to T_IS + C_tr (the T_II and C_aw terms belong
to the near weight form of the near check): for a cut mu_r >= 2 the high-frequency part of T_IS + C_tr is bounded by

    phi mu_r^(-2) [K u_3 + (1 + q) 2^(q+1) T_(q+2)(ubar; mu_r/2)] ||g||^2,
    phi = (1 + 5^(1/2))/2,   K = c_tr' + 5q/2,   c_tr' = 4 (sinh(q log 2) - q sinh(log 2))/(log 2)^3,
    u_3 = m_3(ubar) = (2 pi)^(-2) int |zeta|^3 |ubarhat(zeta)| d zeta,

with the nonnegative tail T_s(f; rho) = (2 pi)^(-2) int_(|zeta| > rho) |zeta|^s |fhat(zeta)| d zeta. At q = 21/10,
c_tr' = 5.42804021782157... and K = 10.67804021782157... (arb, 256 bits; every comparison uses the exact expression).
The T_IS kernel of the lemma is -(q/2) Shat(zeta) d(xi, eta) with d = (n(xi) - n(eta))^2 + (p(xi) - p(eta))^2 <= 2
|zeta|^2/mu_r^2 on max(|xi|, |eta|) >= mu_r (AT-362). With ||Shat(zeta)||_op <= |zeta| |ubarhat(zeta)| this gives the
coefficient q in place of 5q/2, so the bound holds with K_q = c_tr' + q = 7.52804021782157... (AT-362). For a planar
divergence-free ubar, ||Shat(zeta)||_op = |zeta| |ubarhat(zeta)|/2 (AT2564; Corollary (c)), so the T_IS term costs
phi (q/2) u_3/mu_r^2 and the bound holds with K_half = c_tr' + q/2 = 6.47804021782157...; the transport term c_tr',
the tail and all other hypotheses are unchanged (AT2565).

Statement. Let R > 0 be a stipulated rate budget, mu_r >= 2, and K' one of K, K_q and K_half (the last for a planar
divergence-free ubar). (i) If phi mu_r^(-2) [K' u_3 + (1 + q) 2^(q+1) T_(q+2)(ubar; mu_r/2)] <= R, then u_3 <= R
mu_r^2/(phi K'). (ii) If in addition u_3 >= L for a number L > 0, then mu_r >= (phi K' L/R)^(1/2). (iii) If ubarhat
has a finite third moment and the nonunitary inverse formula holds, then |(e.grad)^3 ubar_k(X)| <= u_3 for every unit
vector e, component k and point X; so a certified lower of one third directional derivative at one point is a valid
L.

Proof. (i) The tail is nonnegative, so phi K' u_3/mu_r^2 <= R. (ii) By (i), L <= u_3 <= R mu_r^2/(phi K'); all
quantities are positive. (iii) (e.grad)^3 ubar_k(X) = (2 pi)^(-2) int (i e.zeta)^3 ubarhat_k(zeta) exp(i X.zeta) d
zeta, with |e.zeta|^3 <= |zeta|^3 and |ubarhat_k| <= |ubarhat|. QED.

Corollary. At 013 take R = beta_* - b0 - e_up at the printed near tuples of NEAR_CHECK_v05 to v07 (the certified (R4)
inputs; D4 to D6 of a_n FLOAT), with beta_* the certified whole-plane lower 704955805507/10^12 (AT2535; at version 21
the inner regional lower, the same number) or the ceiling c + 1 - q. All values below are directed 12-place displays
of arb balls at 256 bits with exact rational R and L (theory_agent_1/research/h4rest/hi_kq_v01.py to hi_kq_v04.py, outputs and
SHA256SUMS_v02 to SHA256SUMS_v05 in that directory; each run on core 40 at nice 19 with one thread: hi_kq_v01 and
hi_kq_v02 at the recorded times 10:12:11Z and 10:18:39Z, hi_kq_v03 from 10:25:33.60Z to 10:25:37.38Z (3.78 s),
hi_kq_v04 from 10:37:08.86Z to 10:37:08.98Z), and the theory auditor reproduces every one by an independent 512-bit evaluation
(AT2465, AT2496, AT2510, AT2542, AT2566, AT2601). The ceiling is a necessary global upper, not an attained rate;
using it needs an additional principal or joint estimate (audit_v760).
(a) With K and the numerical auditor's exact axis lower L = 1024389316421/10^12 (numerical audit exchange v725; AT2457, under finite Fourier
    moments and valid nonunitary inverse derivative formulas). At the plan of record (11/20, 72) and the whole-plane
    rate the least cut lies in [138.282830152122, 138.282830152123] at the (R4) version 2 tuple (NEAR_CHECK_v07:
    e_up = 154030238822/10^12, R = 185113337/200000000000; AT2496) and in [140.055012371285, 140.055012371286] at the
    version 1 tuple (NEAR_CHECK_v05, v06: e_up = 154053513894/10^12, R = 902291613/10^12; AT2465, AT2510, AT2542). At
    the version 1 tuple the u_3 ceilings have the directed uppers 0.270727716958 at mu_r 72 and 0.522237108330 at 100
    (AT2465), and the ceiling is about 1.02358473 at 140 and 2.08894843 at 200, so 72, 100 and 140 are excluded there
    and 200 is not (AT2510). At the version 2 tuple the ceiling is below L at mu_r 138 and above it at 139. The other
    least cuts at the version 1 tuple are 53.148063587979 (plan, ceiling rate), 46.837794147744 (fallback (103/200,
    68), regional rate) and 36.300763637682 (fallback, ceiling rate) (the theory auditor
    research/h4_screen_and_origin_cutoffs_audit/DIRECTED_CUTOFF_TESTS_v01.md), and at the version 2 tuple
    53.049623668932, 46.770377422336 and 36.269351312275 (hi_kq_v02.out; AT2566). At the FLOAT tuple of
    NEAR_CHECK_v03 the plan least cut is at least 137.311617637995 (AT2465).
(b) With K_q and L. At the plan of record and the whole-plane rate the least cut lies in [116.108348710641,
    116.108348710642] at the version 2 tuple (the u_3 ceiling is below L at mu_r 116 and above it at 117) and in
    [117.596350878770, 117.596350878771] at the version 1 tuple; at the version 2 tuple the other least cuts are
    44.542798242881 (plan, ceiling rate), 39.270466804208 (fallback, whole-plane rate) and 30.453343231707 (fallback,
    ceiling rate) (hi_kq_v01.out, hi_kq_v02.out; AT2566).
(c) The origin lower L2 < 2 ||Delta S(0)||_op. For ubar = (-Psi_y, Psi_x) and S = Sym D ubar the nonunitary transform
    gives |ubarhat(zeta)| = |zeta| |Psihat(zeta)| and Shat(zeta) = Psihat(zeta) B(zeta), B = [[zeta_1 zeta_2,
    (zeta_2^2 - zeta_1^2)/2], [(zeta_2^2 - zeta_1^2)/2, -zeta_1 zeta_2]], a real symmetric traceless matrix with
    ||B||_op^2 = zeta_1^2 zeta_2^2 + (zeta_2^2 - zeta_1^2)^2/4 = |zeta|^4/4 (checked exactly in hi_kq_v03.py). Hence
    ||Shat(zeta)||_op = |zeta| |ubarhat(zeta)|/2, the Frobenius norm is |zeta| |ubarhat(zeta)|/2^(1/2), and u_3 =
    m_4(Psi) = 2 m_2(S) (the first numerics agent's notes 10:23Z; numerical audit exchange v761; CONFIRMED, AT2564, with the induced complex
    operator norm, finite moments and valid inverse formulas). Then ||Delta S(X)||_op <= m_2(S) at every X, so
    the numerical auditor's exact lower ||Delta S(0)||_op >= 1899576001577/10^12 (AT2456) gives u_3 >= L2 = 3799152003154/10^12,
    about 3.7087 times L (AT2564); if u_3 is infinite, the HI' upper cannot fit at all. With L2 at the version 2
    tuple: at the plan of record and the whole-plane rate the least cut lies in [266.304848871307, 266.304848871308]
    with K and in [223.601268661262, 223.601268661263] with K_q (the ceilings are below L2 at mu_r 266 and above it
    at 267 with K, below at 223 and above at 224 with K_q, and below at 140 and 200 with both); at the plan and the
    ceiling rate 102.162878777455 (K) and 85.780448240223 (K_q); at the fallback and the whole-plane rate
    90.070316592505 (K) and 75.627000950848 (K_q); at the fallback and the ceiling rate 69.847457629053 (K) and
    58.646998749044 (K_q) (hi_kq_v03.out; AT2566).

Remarks.
1. Scope (AT2439, AT2464, AT2569). (i) and (ii) are necessary conditions for that nonnegative sufficient upper to
   fit, not for the actual form. Meeting them proves nothing, since the tail and the other rest pieces share R. They
   exclude nothing at a larger cut, at the ceiling rate, at the fallback, with a better rate, with a different
   estimate or with a coefficient split (AT2466 OPEN).
2. The cut mu_r of the H4-REST pieces may differ from the near cut mu of the near check (Theorem H4-R of the second theory agent), so
   the Corollary does not restrict the near mu. A larger mu_r moves cost from the rate room into the low-frequency
   price, charged against the price allowance (budget (C) of theory_v21 V21-1 (c)).
3. A FLOAT derivative sample gives no certified lower and no excluded cut (AT2441); only a containing evaluation can
   raise L. The first numerics agent's sampled sup ||Delta S||_op = 2.56161 (numerics_v17 N49.5; FLOAT) would give u_3 >= 5.12321
   through (c) and a K_half cut of about 241 at the plan of record (HEURISTIC). Conversely, with a certified upper
   u_3 <= u^+ (for example by Lemma MOM, AT-363, in the form of NR-33 Remark 3), the principal part phi K' u_3/mu_r^2
   fits in R for mu_r >= (phi K' u^+/R)^(1/2); the tail and the other pieces still need their share. With the first numerics agent's
   FLOAT evaluations u^+ = 161.4684 of the Lemma MOM expression (numerics_v17 N49.3) and u^+ = 21.3616 of the
   expression of Lemma MOM dilated by a length a > 0, m_(2j)(f) <= ||h - a Delta h||_2/(4 pi a)^(1/2) with h =
   Delta^j f for a nonnegative integer j, minimized over a (numerics_v17 N49.4, the corrected longdouble production
   run), this would be about 1352 and 492 at the plan of record and about 457 and 166 at the fallback, with K_half
   and the whole-plane rate (HEURISTIC; no certified upper of u_3 exists). The dilated form is the Cauchy-Schwarz
   step of Lemma MOM with the weight (1 + a |zeta|^2)^(-1), int (1 + a |zeta|^2)^(-2) d zeta = pi/a, and Plancherel;
   its square is (A/a + 2B + aC)/(4 pi) with A = ||h||_2^2, B = ||grad h||_2^2, C = ||Delta h||_2^2, minimal at a =
   (A/C)^(1/2) with value (B + (AC)^(1/2))/(2 pi); the theory auditor CONFIRMS it with ordinary Fourier representatives,
   full-plane finite energies and the zero cases retained (AT2588, AT2602). The FLOAT inputs are not certified
   energies (AT2590 OPEN).
4. Hypotheses of (c) and (iii) (the first numerics agent's notes 10:41Z). Finite moments means that the transform has a measurable density
   away from zero with the stated moment finite and that the inverse formula holds; this excludes polynomial parts
   such as Psi = x^3 y, whose moments vanish while ||Delta S||_op = 6. For the 013 fields the first numerics agent reports D^alpha Psi =
   O(r^(1 + 1/c - |alpha|)) at infinity from the finite atom representation, so the fields Delta^2 Psi, Delta^3 Psi
   and Delta^3 Phi_c lie in L^1 and H^2 and the moments used are finite (the first numerics agent's statement; not checked here).
5. Directed thresholds (version 24; the second theory agent's erratum E-H4R02-1, the second theory agent's notes 11:22Z and T2_PART7_v09 section 5.2;
   research/h4rest/u3_thresholds_v01.py of agent the second theory agent; CONFIRMED, AT2658). Read backwards, (i) says: if the HI' upper
   with K_half fits at the cut mu_r, then u_3 <= R mu_r^2/(phi K_half) = L2 (mu_r/mu_0)^2, where mu_0 = (phi K_half

## NR-35. T1-Rule-M4MIN: the direct moment bound in the near budget (PROVED: AT2890 to AT2892)

Status. PROVED (version 27; stated in theory_agent_1/research/closure_check/NEAR_CHECK_v09.md, the first theory agent's note 116). The theory auditor CONFIRMS
the bound replacement of (a) under the admitted NR-25 and near-localization hypotheses (AT2890), the conditional
scalar tuple of (b) by an independent 1152-bit evaluation with both sides of the eight grid transitions (AT2891) and
the cut intervals, moment thresholds, trial decisions and least integers of (c) (AT2892); audit_v878,
research/near_check_v09_audit. That the improvement completes H4 or stationary existence is OPEN (AT2893). The numerical auditor's
independent 768-bit check, importing no producer arithmetic, CONFIRMS (a) and (b) (107 scalar and 28 binding checks;
numerical audit exchange v922, research/near_direct_tuple_audit) and (c) (175 scalar and 28 binding checks; the numerical audit exchange
v923, research/near_direct_hi_cutoffs), and the numerical auditor adopts the theory auditor's review (numerical audit exchange v925). The theory auditor's audit of
version 27 CONFIRMS (a) again under all its imported hypotheses (AT2905) and (b) and (c), with all 24 cut intervals,
20 threshold intervals and 60 trial decisions, by an independent 1152-bit evaluation (AT2906; audit_v882,
research/tnr_v27_integration_audit of agent the theory auditor); AT2909 is OPEN.

Purpose. Section 14 of the prompt asks to optimize the closure parameters. The H4 rate test of the near check leaves
R = beta_* - b0 - e_up for all full-L^2 rest losses (NR-34), and e_up contains the moment term A4c M4(a_n)/mu^4. The
near checks up to version 8 bound M4(a_n) only through the core split of NR-30 (iv). At the certified whole-plane
norms the bound of NR-25 (iii) applied to a_n itself is smaller, and taking the minimum lowers e_up and enlarges R at
both tuples, with no change of the near price, the allowance or any hypothesis.

Imported. NR-25 (iii) (PROVED; AT-892): for real a on R^2 with finite D_m = ||D^m a|| (m = 4, 5, 6) and the
hypotheses of NR-25, M4(a) <= M4^+(D4, D5, D6) := ((D4 D6 + D5^2)/(2 pi))^(1/2). NR-30 (ii) and (iv) (PROVED;
AT-1328): M4(a_c) = 1024 c for the radial core a_c = c/(1 + 4|x|^2), and M4(a) <= 1024 c + M4(a - a_c). The radial
identity D_m(a_n + a_c)^2 <= D_m(a_n)^2 - K_m with the exact core lowers K_m of the near check (AT-1344 to AT-1346,
AT-1454). The certified whole-plane uppers of D4, D5 and D6 of a_n (S.5 item 3) and the other inputs of
NEAR_CHECK_v08.

Statement. (a) Let a_n be the near coefficient at 013, a_r = a_n + a_c (NR-30 Remark 5), and D_m^+ >= ||D^m a_n|| for
m = 4, 5, 6 uppers with (D_m^+)^2 >= K_m. Put D_m^r = ((D_m^+)^2 - K_m)^(1/2). Under the hypotheses of NR-25 for a_n
and for a_r,

    M4(a_n) <= min(1024 c + M4^+(D_4^r, D_5^r, D_6^r), M4^+(D_4^+, D_5^+, D_6^+)),

and the right side is nondecreasing in each D_m^+ and nonincreasing in each K_m, so the interface rule of the near
check (every term monotone in each upper and lower) is kept.
(b) At the inputs of NEAR_CHECK_v08 the split upper is 3.29387563990e3 and the direct upper 2.69196367334e3 (the
    NR-25 output of the adopted whole-plane result is M4 < 2691.963673338907009954203108, AT2871), so the minimum is
    the direct one. The near check with this one changed term (the first theory agent research/closure_check/near_check_v04.py,
    parameters near_params_v06.json unchanged, output near_check_v09.out and near_check_v09.result.json,
    SHA256SUMS_v09; run_nc09.sh on core 41 at nice 19 with one thread from 14:13:11.99Z to 14:13:12.16Z,
    byte-identical to a first run at 14:10:46Z) gives at the plan of record (11/20, 72) the M4 term 6.27517100611e-3
    (7.67827334295e-3 in version 8), e_up = 152627111489/10^12 and R = 1164347009/(5*10^11) = 2.328694018e-3, 2.5159
    times the room of NEAR_CHECK_v08, and at the fallback (103/200, 68) the M4 term 7.88714636483e-3
    (9.65067973862e-3), e_up = 180101237602/10^12 and R = 1970913581/(2*10^11) = 9.854567905e-3, 1.2180 times. So the
    H4 rate test needs beta_* >= 702627111489/10^12 + eps_rest (plan) and beta_* >= 347550618801/(5*10^11) + eps_rest
    (fallback). The cap-level margins become 7.69209871099e-3 and 1.52179725979e-2, the one-variable D6(a_n)
    thresholds 64981.4 and 66304.7 (cap level) and 63629.8 and 65142.9 (H4 rate), and the Lambda_+ thresholds 35.706
    and 25.427 (plan) and 46.979 and 37.810 (fallback); the near price, the allowance and every other term are those
    of version 8. At the plan of record eps_rest is the only input without a certified value; at the fallback the mu
    68 kernel constants and the allowance are also UNAUDITED.
(c) With the rule of NR-34 (ii) at these rooms (the first theory agent research/h4rest/hi_kq_v06.py, output hi_kq_v06.out,
    SHA256SUMS_v06; run from 14:13:12.16Z to 14:13:12.26Z; the program reproduces hi_kq_v05.out on the version 8
    result and the values of NR-34 Corollary (d) on version 7), the least cuts at the plan of record are
    [167.890729992411, 167.890729992412] with K, [140.968444179213, 140.968444179214] with K_q and [130.768385322863,
    130.768385322864] with K_half at L = 3799152003154/10^12, and [194.964026128795, 194.964026128796],
    [163.700374854132, 163.700374854133] and [151.855500860944, 151.855500860945] at u = 5123210549166463/10^15; at
    the fallback [81.613901212275, 81.613901212276], [68.526622511025, 68.526622511026] and [63.568239186948,
    63.568239186949] at L and [94.774588026046, 94.774588026047], [79.576914236786, 79.576914236787] and
    [73.818964551323, 73.818964551324] at u. The least integer cuts at u are 195, 164 and 152 (plan) and 95, 80 and
    74 (fallback). The K_half necessary u_3 uppers lie in [5.132965, 5.132966], [5.975425, 5.975426], [8.447931,
    8.447932], [9.611868, 9.611869], [11.147492, 11.147493] and [12.903728, 12.903729] at mu_r 152, 164, 195, 208,
    224 and 241 (plan) and in [4.873840, 4.873841], [5.148369, 5.148370], [5.430420, 5.430421], [6.017086, 6.017087],
    [6.321701, 6.321702], [7.280675, 7.280676] and [8.485032, 8.485033] at 72, 74, 76, 80, 82, 88 and 95 (fallback).
    At u the trial 152 is excluded with K and K_q and 164 with K, and none of 195, 208, 224 and 241 is excluded
    (plan); 72 is excluded with K, K_q and K_half, 74 and 76 with K and K_q, 80, 82 and 88 with K, and 95 with none
    (fallback). So against Remark 8 the plan trials 208, 224 and 241 are no longer excluded with any of K, K_q and
    K_half, and at the fallback 76 is no longer excluded with K_half and 82 with K_q.

Proof. (a) M4 and the norms ||D^m .|| are invariant under an overall sign, so NR-30 (iv) applied to a = -a_n, with
a - a_c = -a_r, gives M4(a_n) <= 1024 c + M4(a_r), and NR-25 (iii) for a_r with ||D^m a_r|| <= D_m^r bounds M4(a_r)
by M4^+(D_4^r, D_5^r, D_6^r), since M4^+ is nondecreasing in each argument. NR-25 (iii) for a_n with ||D^m a_n|| <=
D_m^+ gives M4(a_n) <= M4^+(D_4^+, D_5^+, D_6^+). Both are uppers of the same number, so their minimum is one. D_m^r
is nondecreasing in D_m^+ and nonincreasing in K_m, and the minimum of two functions that are nondecreasing
(nonincreasing) in a variable is again so. (b) and (c) are directed arb evaluations (256 bits) of the formulas of
NEAR_CHECK_v08 with the one changed term, and of NR-34 (ii) at the rooms of (b), with every decision by exact
rational comparison of displayed values. QED.

Remarks.
1. The split loses because |a_n^| <= a_c^ + |a_r^| pays the full core moment 1024 c (about 1853.77) on top of an
   upper of M4(a_r); at the certified norms the split upper exceeds the direct one by the factor 1.2236. The tail
   terms of e_up keep the split (NR-25 (i) at D6(a_r) and NR-32); a direct tail bound for a_n is not tested here.
2. NR-35 enlarges budgets for an H4 remainder that has no certified bound. It gives no bound of any H4-REST piece, no
   uniform inverse, no invariant ball and no existence, and passing a necessary cut test in (c) certifies no moment
   upper (AT2893; numerical audit exchange v922, v923).
